# Generate a Land-Use Suitability Table

This function takes the path to a raster file containing land-use
suitability values and converts it to a data frame. It filters the data
to include only the cells that have a value of 1 in at least one of the
land-use categories, indicating suitability.

## Usage

``` r
generate_landuse_table(path)
```

## Arguments

- path:

  A string representing the file path to the raster file containing
  land-use suitability data.

## Value

A data frame with the raster cells that have a suitability value of 1 in
at least one of the land-use categories. The data frame includes the
`cell` identifier and the corresponding suitability values for each
land-use category.

## Examples

``` r
# Get path for habitat suitability raster
HabSut <- system.file("ex/HabSut.tif", package = "SpeciesPoolR")

# Use the function to get the data frame
generate_landuse_table(path = HabSut)
```
